  public
  inheriting from ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC
  create public .

public section.

  types:
    tt_email TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF AD_SMTPADR .

  methods SEND_MAIL
    importing
      !I_APPROVER type STRING optional
      !I_REASON type STRING optional
      !I_BUYER type STRING optional
      !I_NFAID type CHAR10 optional
      !I_EMAILTO type TT_EMAIL optional
      !I_EMAILCC type TT_EMAIL optional
      !I_SUBJECT type STRING optional
      !I_CONTENT type STRING optional
    exporting
      !RETURN type CHAR10 .

  methods /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~CREATE_DEEP_ENTITY

CLASS ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT IMPLEMENTATION.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~CREATE_DEEP_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [<---] ER_DEEP_ENTITY                 TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~create_deep_entity.

    types : begin of ty_data.
              include type    ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC=>ts_headerdetails.
    types :   HeaderItemnav        type table of ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC=>ts_itemdetails with default key,
              HeaderToQueryNP      type table of  ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC=>ts_queryset with default key,
              HeaderToAttachmentNP type table of ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC=>ts_wnfaattachment with default key,
            end of ty_data.

    data : nr_range_nr       type  inri-nrrangenr value '01',
           object            type  inri-object  value 'ZWNFA',
           toyear            type  inri-toyear value '2026',
           number            type char10,
           returncode        type  inri-returncode,
           ls_wnfa           type zrfq_wnfa,
           lt_wnfa           type table of zrfq_wnfa,
           ls_hist           type zwnfa_hist,
           lt_item           type table of zrfq_wnfa,
           lt_data           type ty_data,
           lv_level          type char02,
           lv_fail           type char1,
           lv_return         type char10,
           lt_emailto        type table of ad_smtpadr,
           lt_emailcc        type table of ad_smtpadr,
           lv_nfaid          type char10,
           lv_approver       type string,
           lv_buyer          type string, lo_facade         type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid        type string,
           lv_logintype      type char1,
           lt_request_header type tihttpnvp,
           ls_request_header like line of lt_request_header,
           it_query          type table of zgnfa_query_tab,
           wa_query          like line of it_query,
           lv_len            type i,
           lv_len1           type i.

        DATA : HEADER TYPE  THEAD,
      LINES TYPE TABLE OF   TLINE,
      LS_LINES LIKE LINE OF LINES.
DATA: lv_offset TYPE i,
     lv_len5    TYPE i,
     ls_line   TYPE tline,
     lt_lines  TYPE STANDARD TABLE OF tline.



    Lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).
    if lo_facade is bound.
      lt_request_header = lo_facade->get_request_header( ).
    endif.
    read table lt_request_header into ls_request_header with key name = 'loginid'.
    if sy-subrc = 0.
      lv_loginid = ls_request_header-value.
      translate lv_loginid to upper case.
    endif.

    io_data_provider->read_entry_data( importing es_data = lt_data ).
    if lt_data-button = 'DRAFT' or lt_data-button = 'SUBMIT'.
      select emailid from zrfq_user_master into @data(lv_user_emailid) up to 1 rows
        where id =  @lt_data-buyer
        order by emailid.
      endselect.
      if lv_user_emailid is initial.
        raise exception type /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
          exporting
            textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
            message = text-001.
      endif.
      if lt_data-nfaid is initial.

        call function 'NUMBER_GET_NEXT'
          exporting
            object                  = object
            nr_range_nr             = nr_range_nr
            toyear                  = toyear
          importing
            number                  = number
            returncode              = returncode
          exceptions
            object_not_found        = 01
            interval_not_found      = 02
            number_range_not_intern = 03
            others                  = 04.
        if sy-subrc <> 0.
          raise exception type /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
            exporting
              textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
              message = text-002.
        endif.

        lt_data-nfaid = number.
      endif.

      loop at lt_data-headeritemnav into data(ls_item).
        ls_wnfa-nfaid       =  lt_data-nfaid.
        ls_wnfa-buyer = lt_data-buyer.
        ls_wnfa-subject = lt_data-title.

*        lv_len = strlen( lt_data-scope ).
*
*        if lv_len > 255.
*          ls_wnfa-scope = lt_data-scope+0(255).       " First 255 chars
*          ls_wnfa-scope2 = lt_data-scope+255.          " Remaining chars
*        else.
*          ls_wnfa-scope = lt_data-scope.
*          clear ls_wnfa-scope2.
*        endif.
*        clear lv_len.
*        ls_wnfa-scope  = lt_data-scope.

""ADDED BY NAGENDRA 02.07.2026.



HEADER-tdobject = 'TEXT'.
HEADER-tdname = |{ 'WNFAS-' && lt_data-nfaid }|.
CONDENSE HEADER-tdname.
HEADER-tdid    = 'ST'.
HEADER-tdspras    = 'EN'.


lv_len5 = strlen( lt_data-scope ).
WHILE lv_offset < lv_len5.
 CLEAR ls_line.
 ls_line-tdformat = '*'.
 IF lv_len5 - lv_offset >= 132.
   ls_line-tdline = lt_data-scope+lv_offset(132).
 ELSE.
   ls_line-tdline = lt_data-scope+lv_offset.
 ENDIF.
 APPEND ls_line TO lt_lines.
 lv_offset = lv_offset + 132.
ENDWHILE.


call function 'SAVE_TEXT'
  exporting
   CLIENT                  = SY-MANDT
    header                  = HEADER
  tables
    lines                   = lt_lines
  exceptions
    id                      = 1
    language                = 2
    name                    = 3
    object                  = 4
    others                  = 5
          .
if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
endif.

REFRESH : lt_lines.
CLEAR : HEADER , LS_LINE ,lv_offset, lv_len5.
""""END""


*        ls_wnfa-justification = lt_data-justification.
""ADDED BY NAGENDRA 02.07.2026""""
HEADER-tdobject = 'TEXT'.
HEADER-tdname = |{ 'WNFAJ-' && lt_data-nfaid }|.
CONDENSE HEADER-tdname.
HEADER-tdid    = 'ST'.
HEADER-tdspras    = 'EN'.

lv_len5 = strlen( lt_data-justification ).
WHILE lv_offset < lv_len5.
 CLEAR ls_line.
 ls_line-tdformat = '*'.
 IF lv_len5 - lv_offset >= 132.
   ls_line-tdline = lt_data-justification+lv_offset(132).
 ELSE.
   ls_line-tdline = lt_data-justification+lv_offset.
 ENDIF.
 APPEND ls_line TO lt_lines.
 lv_offset = lv_offset + 132.
ENDWHILE.


call function 'SAVE_TEXT'
  exporting
   CLIENT                  = SY-MANDT
    header                  = HEADER
  tables
    lines                   = lt_lines
  exceptions
    id                      = 1
    language                = 2
    name                    = 3
    object                  = 4
    others                  = 5
          .
if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
endif.

REFRESH : lt_lines.
CLEAR : HEADER , LS_LINE ,lv_offset ,lv_len5.

""""END""




        ls_wnfa-grp_details = lt_data-grpdet.
        ls_wnfa-deviation = lt_data-deviations.
        ls_wnfa-vdd = lt_data-vdd.
        ls_wnfa-createdby  = sy-uname.
        ls_wnfa-createdon  = sy-datlo.
        ls_wnfa-createdat  = sy-uzeit.
        if lt_data-button = 'DRAFT'.
          ls_wnfa-draft = 'X'.
          ls_wnfa-status   = 'D'.
          ls_wnfa-alevel   = ''.
        else.
          ls_wnfa-draft = ''.
          ls_wnfa-status   = 'UA'.
          ls_wnfa-alevel   = '0'.
        endif.
        ls_wnfa-po_number       = ls_item-pono.
        ls_wnfa-vendor_name     = ls_item-vendorname.
        ls_wnfa-po_amount       = ls_item-poamt.
        ls_wnfa-nfa_amount      = ls_item-nfaamt.
        ls_wnfa-plant_code      = ls_item-plantcode.
        ls_wnfa-plant_name      = ls_item-plantname.
        ls_wnfa-purchase_grp    = |{ ls_item-purchasegrp alpha = in }|.
        ls_wnfa-del_date        = ls_item-deliverydate.
        ls_wnfa-actaul_com_date = ls_item-actualword.
        ls_wnfa-term_cond       = ls_item-termcond.
        ls_wnfa-work_type       = ls_item-worktype.
        ls_wnfa-amount          = ls_item-amount.
        ls_wnfa-warranty        = ls_item-warrantydlp.
        ls_wnfa-remarks         = ls_item-remarks.
        ls_wnfa-vendor_status   = ls_item-vendorstatus.
        if ls_item-amount <> '0'.

          data :     lv_percentage type p decimals 2.

          lv_percentage   = ( ls_item-amount / ls_item-poamt )  * 100 .
          ls_wnfa-contract_val   = lv_percentage.

*          shift ls_wnfa-contract_val left deleting leading '0'.
        endif.
        append ls_wnfa to lt_wnfa.
        clear ls_wnfa.
      endloop.
      data : gs_nfaatt      type zrfq_attachment,
             ls_nfaatt      type zrfq_attachment,
             lt_nfaatt      type table of zrfq_attachment,
             lv_dokar       type draw-dokar,
             lv_doknr       type draw-doknr,
             lv_dokvr       type dokvr,
             lv_doktl       type draw-doktl value '000',
             dokar          type draw-dokar,
             doknr          type draw-doknr,
             dokvr          type dokvr,
             doktl          type draw-doktl,
             lv_sernr       type numc2,
             lv_create_flag type flag,
             lv_file_name   type filep.

      loop at lt_data-headertoattachmentnp into data(nfaatt).
        if nfaatt-docid is initial.
          select * from zrfq_attachment into table @data(it_nfaatt) where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
            and doknr = @nfaatt-docid.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            sort it_nfaatt by dokvr descending.
            read table it_nfaatt into ls_NFAATT index 1.
            if sy-subrc = 0.
              lv_doknr = ls_NFAATT-doknr.
              lv_dokvr = ls_NFAATT-dokvr.
            endif.
          endif.

          if lv_dokvr is not initial and lv_dokvr ne '00'.
            lv_sernr = lv_dokvr.
            lv_sernr = lv_sernr + 1.
          else.
            lv_sernr = '01'.
          endif.
          lv_dokvr = lv_sernr.
          lv_create_flag = 'X'.
          lv_file_name = nfaatt-filename.

          lv_dokar = 'ZVP'.
          try.
              call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>send_file_to_dms
                exporting
                  mimetype    = nfaatt-filetype
                  content     = nfaatt-filecontent
                  file_name   = lv_file_name
                  create_flag = lv_create_flag
                  i_doknr     = lv_doknr
                  i_dokvr     = lv_dokvr
                  i_dokar     = lv_dokar
                importing
                  dokar       = dokar
                  doknr       = doknr
                  dokvr       = dokvr
                  doktl       = doktl.
            catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
            catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
          endtry.
          nfaatt-docid =   doknr.
          .

          ls_NFAATT-NFAid = lt_data-nfaid.
          ls_NFAATT-dokar        = dokar.
          ls_NFAATT-doknr        = nfaatt-docid .
          ls_NFAATT-dokvr        = dokvr.
          ls_NFAATT-doktl        = doktl.
          ls_nfaatt-quotationid    = nfaatt-qutationid.
          ls_NFAATT-file_name    = lv_file_name.
          ls_NFAATT-uploaded_by  = lv_loginid.
          ls_NFAATT-dele_ind     = ''.
          ls_NFAatt-specification =  nfaatt-specification.
          ls_NFAatt-attachmenttype = nfaatt-attachmenttype.
          ls_NFAatt-file_type =      nfaatt-filetype.
          ls_NFAatt-sendtosupplier = nfaatt-sendtosupplier.
          append ls_NFAatt to lt_nfaatt.
          clear ls_nfaatt.
        else.


          select * from zrfq_attachment into @Gs_NFAatt up to 1 rows
            where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid and doknr = @nfaatt-docid
            order by dokvr descending.
          endselect.

          ls_NFAATT-NFAid = lt_data-nfaid.
          ls_NFAATT-dokar        = Gs_NFAatt-dokar.
          ls_NFAATT-doknr        = nfaatt-docid .
          ls_NFAATT-dokvr        = Gs_NFAatt-dokvr.
          ls_NFAATT-doktl        = Gs_NFAatt-doktl.
          ls_nfaatt-quotationid    = nfaatt-qutationid.
          ls_NFAATT-file_name    = Gs_NFAatt-file_name.
          ls_NFAATT-uploaded_by  = lv_loginid.
          ls_NFAATT-dele_ind     = ''.
          ls_NFAatt-specification = gs_NFAatt-specification.
          ls_NFAatt-attachmenttype = gs_NFAatt-attachmenttype.
          ls_NFAatt-file_type = gs_NFAatt-file_type.
          ls_NFAatt-sendtosupplier = nfaatt-sendtosupplier.
          append ls_NFAatt to lt_nfaatt.
          clear ls_nfaatt.
        endif.


*      if ls_RFQATT is not initial.
*         MODIFY zrfq_attachment from ls_rfqatt.
        clear: dokar, dokvr, doknr, doktl, lv_doknr, lv_dokvr , nfaatt.
*      endif.

        nfaatt-filecontent = ' '.
        modify lt_data-headertoattachmentnp from nfaatt transporting filecontent.
      endloop.

      if lt_nfaatt is not initial.
        modify zrfq_attachment from table LT_NFAatt.

      endif.




      if lt_data-button = 'SUBMIT'.
        ls_wnfa = lt_wnfa[ 1 ].
        select single
          level1,
          level2,
          level3,
          level4,
          level5,
          level6,
          level7
          from zgroup_details
          into @data(ls_grp_det)
          where grp_id = @ls_wnfa-grp_details.
        if sy-subrc is initial.
          select single
            hod_user_department,
            hod_scm,
            hod_project_dev,
            functional_hod_scm,
            project_controller,
            budget_controller,
            finance_controller,
            head_sc,
            chro,
            gcfo,
            ceo,
            mgr_scm
            from zdepartment_det
            into @data(ls_department_det)
            where pur_grp = @ls_wnfa-purchase_grp.
          if sy-subrc is initial.
            select
              id,
              name,
              emailid
              from zrfq_user_master
              into table @data(lt_user_master)
              where id = @ls_department_det-hod_user_department
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-hod_scm
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-hod_project_dev
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-project_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-budget_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-finance_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-head_sc
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-chro
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-gcfo
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-ceo
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-mgr_scm
                 or id =  @ls_wnfa-buyer.
            if ls_department_det-hod_user_department is not initial
               and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-U'
                  or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-U'
                  or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-U'
                  or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-U'
                  or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-U'
                  or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-U' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-U').
              read table lt_user_master into data(ls_user_master_check) with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.
              if sy-subrc is not initial or ls_user_master_check-emailid is initial.
                lv_fail = abap_true.
                concatenate text-003 ls_department_det-hod_user_department into data(lv_msg) separated by space.
              endif.
              clear ls_user_master_check.
            elseif ls_department_det-hod_user_department is initial
            and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-U'
               or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-U'
               or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-U'
               or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-U'
               or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-U'
               or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-U' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-U').
              lv_fail = abap_true.
              lv_msg = text-004.
            endif.
            if lv_fail is initial.
              if ls_department_det-hod_scm is not initial
                    and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-S'
                       or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-S'
                       or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-S'
                       or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-S'
                       or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-S'
                       or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-S' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-S') .
                read table lt_user_master into ls_user_master_check with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.
                if sy-subrc is not initial or ls_user_master_check-emailid is initial.
                  lv_fail = abap_true.
                  concatenate text-003 ls_department_det-hod_scm into lv_msg separated by space.
                endif.
                clear ls_user_master_check.
              elseif ls_department_det-hod_scm is initial
                  and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-S'
                     or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-S'
                     or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-S'
                     or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-S'
                     or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-S'
                     or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-S' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-S') .
                lv_fail = abap_true.
                lv_msg = text-005.
              endif.
            endif.
            if lv_fail is initial.
              if ls_department_det-hod_project_dev is not initial
                   and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-PD'
                      or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-PD'
                      or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-PD'
                      or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-PD'
                      or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-PD'
                      or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-PD' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-PD') .
                read table lt_user_master into ls_user_master_check with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.
                if sy-subrc is not initial or ls_user_master_check-emailid is initial.
                  lv_fail = abap_true.
                  concatenate text-003 ls_department_det-hod_project_dev into lv_msg separated by space.
                endif.
                clear ls_user_master_check.
              elseif
                ls_department_det-hod_project_dev is initial
                 and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'HOD-PD'
                    or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'HOD-PD'
                    or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'HOD-PD'
                    or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'HOD-PD'
                    or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'HOD-PD'
                    or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'HOD-PD' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'HOD-PD') .
                lv_fail = abap_true.
                lv_msg = text-006.
              endif.
            endif.
            if lv_fail is initial.
              if ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm is not initial
               and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'FHODSCM'
                  or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'FHODSCM'
                  or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'FHODSCM'
                  or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'FHODSCM'
                  or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'FHODSCM'
                  or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'FHODSCM' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'FHODSCM') .
                read table lt_user_master into ls_user_master_check with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.
                if sy-subrc is not initial or ls_user_master_check-emailid is initial.
                  lv_fail = abap_true.
                  concatenate text-003 ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm into lv_msg separated by space.
                endif.
                clear ls_user_master_check.
              elseif ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm is initial
             and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'FHODSCM'
                or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'FHODSCM'
                or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'FHODSCM'
                or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'FHODSCM'
                or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'FHODSCM'
                or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'FHODSCM' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'FHODSCM') .
                lv_fail = abap_true.
                lv_msg = text-007.
              endif.
            endif.
            if lv_fail is initial.
              if ls_department_det-project_controller is not initial
               and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'PRCTR'
                  or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'PRCTR'

    elseif lt_data-button = 'A' or lt_data-button = 'R'.

      loop at lt_data-headeritemnav into ls_item.
        ls_wnfa-nfaid       =  lt_data-nfaid.
        ls_wnfa-po_number       = ls_item-pono.
        append ls_wnfa to lt_wnfa.
        clear ls_wnfa.
      endloop.
      select
        *
        from zrfq_wnfa
        into table @data(lt_rfq_wnfa)
        for all entries in @lt_wnfa
        where nfaid = @lt_wnfa-nfaid
          and po_number =  @lt_wnfa-po_number.
      if lt_rfq_wnfa is not initial.
        sort lt_rfq_wnfa by nfaid po_number.
        loop at lt_rfq_wnfa assigning <fs_item>.
          if lt_data-button = 'A'.
            <fs_item>-alevel = <fs_item>-alevel + 1.
            lv_level = <fs_item>-alevel.
          else.
            lv_level = <fs_item>-alevel + 1.
            <fs_item>-alevel = ''.
            <fs_item>-status = 'R'.
          endif.
        endloop.
        ls_hist-nfaid = lt_data-nfaid.
        ls_hist-alevel = lv_level.
        ls_hist-action = lt_data-button.
        ls_hist-remark = lt_data-remark.
        ls_hist-edate = sy-datum.
        ls_hist-etime = sy-uzeit.

        ls_wnfa = lt_rfq_wnfa[ 1 ].

        select single
          level1,
          level2,
          level3,
          level4,
          level5,
          level6
          from zgroup_details
          into @ls_grp_det
          where grp_id = @ls_wnfa-grp_details.
        if sy-subrc is initial.
          select single
            hod_user_department,
            hod_scm,
            hod_project_dev,
            functional_hod_scm,
            project_controller,
            budget_controller,
            finance_controller,
            head_sc,
            chro,
            gcfo,
            ceo
            from zdepartment_det
            into @ls_department_det
            where pur_grp = @ls_wnfa-purchase_grp.
          if sy-subrc is initial.
            select
              id,
              name,
              emailid
              from zrfq_user_master
              into table @lt_user_master
              where id = @ls_department_det-hod_user_department
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-hod_scm
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-hod_project_dev
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-project_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-budget_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-finance_controller
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-head_sc
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-chro
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-gcfo
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-ceo
                 or id =  @ls_department_det-mgr_scm
                 or id =  @ls_wnfa-buyer.
            if lt_data-button = 'A'.
              case lv_level.
                when '1'.

              endcase.

    if LT_Data-button = 'Q'.

      select * from  zgnfa_query_tab where nfaid = @lT_data-nfaid
      into table @data(lt_query).

      select single max( counter )
        from zgnfa_query_tab where nfaid = @lT_DATA-nfaid
        into @data(qcounter).
      loop at lT_data-headertoquerynp assigning field-symbol(<query>).
        lv_len = strlen( <query>-question ).
        lv_len1 = strlen( <query>-answer ).

        if lv_len > 255.
          read table lt_query into data(wa_query1) with key
        nfaid = lt_data-nfaid question = <query>-question+0(255)
        question1 = <query>-question+255.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
            set answer = @<query>-answer+0(255),
                answer1 = @<query>-answer+255
            where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
              and question = @wa_query1-question
              and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
              and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
            else.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
                       set answer = @<query>-answer
                       where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid
                         and question = @wa_query1-question
                         and question1 = @wa_query1-question1
                         and counter = @wa_query1-counter.
            endif.
          else.
            qcounter  = qcounter + 1.
            wa_query-nfaid  = LT_dATA-nfaid.
            wa_query-question = <query>-question+0(255).
            wa_query-question1 = <query>-question+255.
            wa_query-counter  = qcounter.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
              wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
            else.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
            endif.
            wa_query-querychk = 'X'.
            if <query>-raisedby is initial.
              select single name from zrfq_user_master
              into @data(raisedbyname) where id = @lv_loginid
              and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
              wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
            else.
              wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
            endif.
            wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
            wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
            wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
            wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
            append wa_query to it_query.
            clear : wa_query.
          endif.
        else.
          read table lt_query into wa_query1 with key
                  nfaid = lt_data-nfaid question = <query>-question.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              update zgnfa_query_tab
              set answer = @<query>-answer+0(255),
              answer1 = @<query>-answer+255
            where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid  and question = @wa_query1-question
            and counter = @wa_query1-counter .
            else.
              update zgnfa_query_tab set answer = @<query>-answer
                        where nfaid = @lt_data-nfaid  and question = @wa_query1-question
                        and counter = @wa_query1-counter .
            endif.
          else.
            qcounter  = qcounter + 1.
            wa_query-nfaid  = LT_dATA-nfaid.
            wa_query-question = <query>-question.
            wa_query-counter  = qcounter.
            if lv_len1 > 255.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer+0(255).
              wa_query-answer1  = <query>-answer+255.
            else.
              wa_query-answer  = <query>-answer.
            endif.
            wa_query-querychk = 'X'.
            if <query>-raisedby is initial.
              select single name from zrfq_user_master
              into @raisedbyname where id = @lv_loginid
              and usertype = 'APPROVER'.
              wa_query-raisedby  = raisedbyname.
            else.
              wa_query-raisedby = <query>-raisedby.
            endif.
            wa_query-raisedon = sy-datum.
            wa_query-crtat  = sy-uzeit.
            wa_query-crtby  = sy-uname.
            wa_query-crton  = sy-datlo .
            append wa_query to it_query.
            clear : wa_query.
          endif.
        endif.
        clear : lv_len, lv_len1.

*        nfaid = lt_data-nfaid question = <query>-question.
*        if sy-subrc = 0.

            read table lt_user_master into ls_user_master with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.
            if ls_user_master-emailid is not initial
               and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'GCFO' or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'GCFO'
                  or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'GCFO' or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'GCFO'
                  or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'GCFO' or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'GCFO' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'GCFO' ).
              append ls_user_master-emailid to lt_emailcc.
            endif.
            clear ls_user_master.
            read table lt_user_master into ls_user_master with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.
            if ls_user_master-emailid is not initial
               and ( ls_grp_det-level1 = 'CEO' or ls_grp_det-level2 = 'CEO'
                  or ls_grp_det-level3 = 'CEO' or ls_grp_det-level4 = 'CEO'
                  or ls_grp_det-level5 = 'CEO' or ls_grp_det-level6 = 'CEO' or ls_grp_det-level7 = 'CEO' ).
              append ls_user_master-emailid to lt_emailcc.
            endif.
            clear ls_user_master.

            "SEND MAIL with lt_emailcc and lt_emailto
            lv_nfaid = lt_data-nfaid.
            lv_approver = raisedbyname.
            me->send_mail(
              exporting
                i_approver = lv_approver
                i_buyer    = lv_buyer
                i_nfaid    = lv_nfaid
                i_emailto  = lt_emailto
                i_emailcc  = lt_emailcc
                i_subject  = text-023
                i_content  = 'MAIL6'
              importing
                return     = lv_return                 " Character Field with Length 10
            ).
          endif.
        endif.
      endif.


    endif.



    lt_data-nfaid = |{ lt_data-nfaid alpha = out }|.
    copy_data_to_ref( exporting is_data = lt_data
                      changing  cr_data = er_deep_entity ).

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_EXPANDED_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_EXPAND                      TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ODATA_EXPAND(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_CLAUSES            TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [<---] ET_EXPANDED_TECH_CLAUSES       TYPE        STRING_TABLE
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /iwbep/if_mgw_appl_srv_runtime~get_expanded_entity.
    data : ls_data type ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC_ext=>ty_data,
           ls_keys type ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mPC_ext=>ts_headerdetails.

    data : lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid   type string,
           lv_logintype type char1,
           lv_userrole  type char1,
           lv_bukrs     type bukrs,
           lv_lifnr     type lifnr,
           lv_level     type char10.

       DATA : ID TYPE  THEAD-TDID,
LANGUAGE  TYPE  THEAD-TDSPRAS,
NAME1  TYPE  THEAD-TDNAME,
OBJECT  TYPE  THEAD-TDOBJECT,
LV_scope TYPE STRING,
lv_justification TYPE  string.

DATA : LINES  TYPE TABLE OF TLINE,
       LS_LINES LIKE LINE OF LINES.

    lo_facade  ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

**-- Get Parameters
    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).

    io_tech_request_context->get_converted_keys(
      importing
        es_key_values = ls_keys ).

    case iv_entity_set_name.
      when 'HeaderDetailsSet'.
        select
          a~po_number,
          a~vendor_name,
          a~po_amount,
          a~nfa_amount,
          a~plant_code,
          a~plant_name,
          a~purchase_grp,
          a~del_date,
          a~actaul_com_date,
          a~term_cond,
          a~work_type,
          a~Amount,
          a~contract_val,
          a~warranty,
          a~Remarks,
          a~vendor_status
          from zrfq_wnfa as a
          into table @data(lT_ITEM)
          where nfaid = @ls_keys-nfaid.
        if lt_item is not initial.
          select ekgrp, eknam from t024 into table @data(lt_t024_grp)
            for all entries in @lt_item
            where ekgrp = @lt_item-purchase_grp.
          loop at lt_item into data(ls_item_chk).
            if not line_exists( lt_t024_grp[ ekgrp = ls_item_chk-purchase_grp ] ).
              delete lt_item.
            endif.
          endloop.
        endif.
        if lt_item is not initial.
          select
            alevel,
            status,
            buyer,
            nfa_type,
            vdd,
            deviation,
            grp_details,
            justification,
            scope,
            scope2,
            subject,
            nfaid,
            purchase_grp
            from zrfq_wnfa
            into @data(ls_ITEM) up to 1 rows
            where nfaid = @ls_keys-nfaid
            order by nfaid.
          endselect.
          if sy-subrc is initial.
            select single
              hod_user_department,
              hod_scm,
              hod_project_dev,
              functional_hod_scm,
              project_controller,
              budget_controller,
              finance_controller,
              head_sc,
              chro,
              gcfo,
              ceo,
              mgr_scm
              from zdepartment_det
              into @data(ls_department_det)
              where pur_grp = @ls_ITEM-purchase_grp.
            if sy-subrc is initial.
              select
                *
                from znfa_apr_deli
                where pur_grp = @ls_ITEM-purchase_grp
                  and startdate <= @sy-datum
                  and enddate >= @sy-datum
                  and deleted is initial
                into table @data(lt_nfa_apr_deli).
              if lt_nfa_apr_deli is not initial.
                loop at lt_nfa_apr_deli into data(ls_nfa_apr_deli).
                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_user_department is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-hod_user_department = ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_user_department.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_user_department is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-hod_user_department = ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_user_department.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_scm is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-hod_scm = ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_scm.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_project_dev is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-hod_project_dev = ls_nfa_apr_deli-hod_project_dev.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-functional_hod_scm is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm = ls_nfa_apr_deli-functional_hod_scm.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-project_controller is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-project_controller = ls_nfa_apr_deli-project_controller.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-budget_controller is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-budget_controller = ls_nfa_apr_deli-budget_controller.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-finance_controller is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-finance_controller = ls_nfa_apr_deli-finance_controller.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-head_sc is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-head_sc = ls_nfa_apr_deli-head_sc.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-chro is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-chro = ls_nfa_apr_deli-chro.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-gcfo is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-gcfo = ls_nfa_apr_deli-gcfo.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-ceo is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-ceo = ls_nfa_apr_deli-ceo.
                  endif.

                  if ls_nfa_apr_deli-mgr_scm is not initial.
                    ls_department_det-mgr_scm = ls_nfa_apr_deli-mgr_scm.
                  endif.
                  CLEAR ls_nfa_apr_deli.
                endloop.
              endif.




ID = 'ST'.
LANGUAGE = 'EN'.
NAME1  = |{ 'WNFAS-' && ls_keys-nfaid }|.
CONDENSE NAME1.
OBJECT = 'TEXT'.

        call function 'READ_TEXT'
          exporting
           CLIENT                        = SY-MANDT
            id                            = ID
            language                      = LANGUAGE
            NAME                          = NAME1
            OBJECT                        = OBJECT
          tables
            lines                         = LINES
         EXCEPTIONS
           ID                            = 1
           LANGUAGE                      = 2
           NAME                          = 3
           NOT_FOUND                     = 4
           OBJECT                        = 5
           REFERENCE_CHECK               = 6
           WRONG_ACCESS_TO_ARCHIVE       = 7
           OTHERS                        = 8
                  .
        if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
        endif.

LOOP AT LINES INTO LS_LINES.
CONCATENATE lv_scope LS_LINES-tdline INTO lv_scope SEPARATED BY space.
ENDLOOP.

*lv_scope = lv_text.
CLEAR :  id , language , name1 , object ,ls_lines.
refresh : lines.



ID = 'ST'.
LANGUAGE = 'EN'.
NAME1  = |{ 'WNFAJ-' && ls_keys-nfaid }|.
CONDENSE NAME1.
OBJECT = 'TEXT'.

        call function 'READ_TEXT'
          exporting
           CLIENT                        = SY-MANDT
            id                            = ID
            language                      = LANGUAGE
            NAME                          = NAME1
            OBJECT                        = OBJECT
          tables
            lines                         = LINES
         EXCEPTIONS
           ID                            = 1
           LANGUAGE                      = 2
           NAME                          = 3
           NOT_FOUND                     = 4
           OBJECT                        = 5
           REFERENCE_CHECK               = 6
           WRONG_ACCESS_TO_ARCHIVE       = 7
           OTHERS                        = 8
                  .
        if sy-subrc <> 0.
* Implement suitable error handling here
        endif.


LOOP AT LINES INTO LS_LINES.
CONCATENATE lv_justification LS_LINES-tdline INTO lv_justification SEPARATED BY space.
ENDLOOP.

*lv_justification = lv_text.
CLEAR :  id , language , name1 , object ,ls_lines.
refresh : lines.



              ls_data-headeritemnav = value #( for ls_item1 in lt_item (
                                               PoNo            = ls_item1-po_number
                                               VendorName      = ls_item1-vendor_name
                                               POAmt           = ls_item1-po_amount
                                               NFAAmt          = ls_item1-nfa_amount
                                               PlantCode       = ls_item1-plant_code
                                               PlantName       = ls_item1-plant_name
                                               PurchaseGrp     = ls_item1-purchase_grp
                                               purchasegrpname = value #( lt_t024_grp[ ekgrp = ls_item1-purchase_grp ]-eknam optional )
                                               DeliveryDate    = ls_item1-del_date
                                               ActualWord      = ls_item1-actaul_com_date
                                               Termcond        = ls_item1-term_cond
                                               WorkType        = ls_item1-work_type
                                               Amount          = ls_item1-Amount
                                               ContractVal     = ls_item1-contract_val
                                               WarrantyDLP     = ls_item1-warranty
                                               Remarks         = ls_item1-Remarks
                                               VendorStatus    = ls_item1-vendor_status ) ).
*                                               VendorStatus    = cond #(
*                                                                 when ls_item1-sperm = ls_item1-sperr
*                                                                 then 'Blocked'
*                                                                 else 'Active' ) ) ).


              ls_data-nfaid = ls_item-nfaid.
              ls_data-nfaid = |{ ls_item-nfaid alpha = out }|.
              ls_data-title = ls_item-subject.
*              ls_data-scope = |{ ls_item-scope }{ ls_item-scope2 }|.
              ls_data-scope = lv_scope.
              ls_data-justification = lv_justification.
              ls_data-grpdet = ls_item-grp_details.
              ls_data-deviations = ls_item-deviation.
              ls_data-vdd = ls_item-vdd.
              ls_data-nfatype = ls_item-nfa_type.

              select id, name from zrfq_user_master into table @data(lt_USER_MASTER)  where id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-hod_user_department+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-hod_scm+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-hod_project_dev+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-functional_hod_scm+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-project_controller+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-budget_controller+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-finance_controller+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-head_sc+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-chro+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-gcfo+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-ceo+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_DEPARTMENT_DET-mgr_scm+0(10)
                                                                                                                  or id = @ls_item-buyer.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into data(ls_name) with key id = ls_item-buyer.
              if sy-subrc is initial.
                ls_data-buyer = ls_name-id.
                ls_data-buyerNAME = ls_name-name.
              endif.
              clear ls_name.
              ls_data-level = ls_item-alevel.
              ls_data-approve = abap_false.
              if ls_item-status <> 'D'.
                select single * from zgroup_details into @data(ls_grp_det) where grp_id = @ls_data-grpdet.
                if sy-subrc is initial.
                  select role, description from zroles into table @data(lt_roles) where role <> @space.
                  if sy-subrc is initial.
                    if ls_grp_det is not initial.
                      case ls_item-alevel.
                        when 0.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level1.
                        when 1.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level2.
                        when 2.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level3.
                        when 3.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level4.
                        when 4.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level5.
                        when 5.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level6.
                        when 6.
                          lv_level = ls_grp_det-level7.
                      endcase.
                      if lv_level is not initial.
                        if ls_DEPARTMENT_DET is not initial.
                          case lv_level.
                            when 'HOD-U'.
                              if ls_department_det-hod_user_department = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'HOD-S'.
                              if ls_department_det-hod_scm = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'HOD-PD'.
                              if ls_department_det-hod_project_dev = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'FHODSCM'.
                              if ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'PRCTR'.
                              if ls_department_det-project_controller = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'BGTCTR'.
                              if ls_department_det-budget_controller = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'FICTR'.
                              if ls_department_det-finance_controller = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'HEADSC'.
                              if ls_department_det-head_sc = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'CHRO'.
                              if ls_department_det-chro = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'GCFO'.
                              if ls_department_det-gcfo = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'CEO'.
                              if ls_department_det-ceo = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                            when 'MGR-SCM'.
                              if ls_department_det-mgr_scm = lv_loginid.
                                ls_data-approve = abap_true.
                              endif.
                          endcase.
                        endif.
                      endif.
                    endif.

                    select * from zwnfa_hist into table @data(lt_hist) where nfaid = @ls_item-nfaid.
                    sort lt_hist by edate descending etime descending.

                    read table lt_ROLES into data(ls_ROLES) with key role = ls_grp_det-level1.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level1 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level1name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into data(ls_hist) with key alevel = 1 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level1date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level1time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES,ls_hist.

                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level2.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level2 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level2name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 2 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level2date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level2time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES,ls_hist.

                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level3.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level3 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level3name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 3 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level3date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level3time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES, ls_hist.



                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level4.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level4 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level4name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 4 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level4date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level4time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES, ls_hist.

                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level5.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level5 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level5name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 5 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level5date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level5time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES, ls_hist.

                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level6.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level6 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level6name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 6 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level6date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level6time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES,ls_hist.

                    read table lt_ROLES into ls_ROLES with key role = ls_grp_det-level7.
                    if sy-subrc is initial.
                      ls_data-level7 = ls_ROLES-description.
                      case ls_ROLES-description.

            when 'HOD-USER DEPARTMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.

            when 'HOD-SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.

            when 'HEAD S&C'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.

            when 'PROJECT CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.

            when 'FINANCE CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.

            when 'GCFO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.

            when 'CEO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.

            when 'HOD-PROJECT DEVELOPMENT'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.

            when 'MANAGER SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.

            when 'BUDGET CONTROLLER'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.

            when 'CHRO'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-chro.

            when 'FUNCTIONAL HOD SCM'.
              read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_name
                   with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.

            when others.
              clear ls_name.

          endcase.

                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level7name = ls_name-name.
                      endif.

                      clear ls_name.
                      read table lt_hist into ls_hist with key alevel = 7 action = 'A'.
                      if sy-subrc = 0.
                        ls_data-level7date = ls_hist-edate.
                        ls_data-level7time = |{ ls_hist-etime+0(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+2(2) }:{ ls_hist-etime+4(2) }|.
                      endif.
                    endif.
                    clear : ls_ROLES,ls_hist.
                  endif.
                endif.
              endif.
              copy_data_to_ref(
                exporting
                  is_data = ls_DATA
                changing
                  cr_data = er_entity ).
            else.
              raise exception type /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception
                exporting
                  textid  = /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception=>business_error
                  message = text-024.
            endif.
          endif.
        endif.
    endcase.
*      endif.
*    endif.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->/IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_STREAM
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH(optional)
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY(optional)
* | [<---] ER_STREAM                      TYPE REF TO DATA
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_ENTITY_CNTXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME~GET_STREAM.
data: ls_key_tab     type /iwbep/s_mgw_name_value_pair,
      PRITMKEY TYPE DRAD-OBJKY,
      DOCID TYPE DOKNR,
      lv_file_name    type char200,
      ls_stream      type ty_s_media_resource,
            ls_header      TYPE ihttpnvp,
     lv_name_utf8    TYPE string,
        lv_filename_str TYPE string.
constants: lc_content_disp TYPE string VALUE 'Content-Disposition',
           lc_attach_utf8  TYPE string VALUE 'attachment; filename*=UTF-8'''''.


CASE iv_entity_set_name.
WHEN  'GetWnfaAttachmentSet'.
read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'NFAID'.
IF sy-subrc = 0.
DATA(NFAID) = ls_key_tab-value.
ENDIF.

  read table it_key_tab into ls_key_tab with key name = 'DOCID'.
IF sy-subrc = 0.
DOCID = ls_key_tab-value.
ENDIF.

SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT
  FIELDS
       DOKAR,
       DOKNR,
       DOKVR,
       DOKTL
  WHERE NFAID = @NFAID AND DOKNR = @DOCID
  ORDER BY DOKVR DESCENDING
  INTO @DATA(LS_RFQATT) UP TO 1 ROWS.
ENDSELECT.

try.
     call method zcl_supp_portal_utility=>retrieve_file_from_dms
              exporting
                dokar     = ls_RFQatt-dokar
                doknr     = ls_RFQatt-doknr
                dokvr     = ls_RFQatt-dokvr
                doktl     = ls_RFQatt-doktl
              importing
                mimetype  = ls_stream-mime_type
                content   = ls_stream-value
                file_name = lv_file_name.
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_busi_exception .
          catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception .
        endtry.
        ENDCASE.


  ls_header-name = lc_content_disp.
          lv_filename_str = lv_file_name.
          lv_name_utf8 = cl_http_utility=>if_http_utility~escape_url( unescaped =  lv_filename_str ).
          REPLACE ALL OCCURRENCES OF '%2e' IN lv_name_utf8 WITH '.'.
          CONCATENATE lc_attach_utf8 lv_name_utf8 INTO ls_header-value.
          set_header( is_header = ls_header ).

        copy_data_to_ref( exporting is_data = ls_stream
                    changing  cr_data = er_stream ).
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->APPROVALDETAILSS_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_APPROVALDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method approvaldetailss_get_entityset.

    data: r_GRP_DET    type range of zde_grp_id,
          ls_GRP_DET   like line of r_GRP_DET,
          es_entityset like line of et_entityset.

    try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'GrpDet' ].
        data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        ls_GRP_DET-low = ls_selopt-low.
        ls_GRP_DET-sign   = 'I'.
        ls_GRP_DET-option = 'CP'.
        append ls_GRP_DET to r_GRP_DET.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

    select * from zgroup_details into TABLE @data(lt_grp_det) where grp_id in @r_GRP_DET.
      LOOP AT lt_grp_det into data(ls_grp).
        es_entityset-nfaid = ls_grp-grp_id.
        es_entityset-level1 = ls_grp-level1.
        es_entityset-level2 = ls_grp-level2.
        es_entityset-level3 = ls_grp-level3.
        es_entityset-level4 = ls_grp-level4.
        es_entityset-level5 = ls_grp-level5.
        es_entityset-level6 = ls_grp-level6.
        APPEND es_entityset to et_entityset.
        CLEAR es_entityset.
      ENDLOOP.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->ATTACHMENT_DDSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_ATTACHMENT_DD
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method ATTACHMENT_DDSET_GET_ENTITYSET.
SELECT FROM ZRFQ_DASHB_DD
  fields DISTINCT
  value_dd as value
  where key_dd = 'NFAAT'
  INTO TABLE @ET_ENTITYSET.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->GRPDETHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_GRPDETHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
