  method grpdethelpset_get_entityset.
    data : lv_search_string type string,
           lt_grp_det       type range of char20,
           ls_grp_det       like line of lt_grp_det.

    if iv_search_string is not initial.
      lv_search_string = iv_search_string.
      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
      concatenate '*' lv_search_string '*' into lv_search_string.

      ls_grp_det-sign = 'I'.
      ls_grp_det-option = 'CP'.
      translate lv_search_string to upper case.
      ls_grp_det-low = lv_search_string.
      append ls_grp_det to lt_grp_det.
      clear ls_grp_det.

    endif.

    if iv_search_string is initial.
      select
        grp_id,
        category,
        type,
        nfa_cond
        from zgroup_details
        into table @et_entityset
        where grp_id in @lt_grp_det.
    else.
      select
        grp_id,
        category,
        type,
        nfa_cond
        from zgroup_details
        into table @et_entityset
        where ( grp_id in @lt_grp_det or category in @lt_grp_det
                or type in @lt_grp_det or nfa_cond in @lt_grp_det ).
    endif.

    sort et_entityset ascending by grpdet.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->HEADERDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_HEADERDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method headerdetailsset_get_entityset.
    data : lr_nfaid      type range of char10,
           lv_level      type char10,
           lv_apppending type string,
           lo_facade     type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
           lv_loginid    type string,
           lv_logintype  type char1,
           lv_userrole   type char1,
           lv_bukrs      type bukrs.

    read table it_filter_select_options transporting no fields with key property = 'NFAID'.
    if sy-subrc is initial and it_filter_select_options is not initial.
      lr_nfaid = corresponding #( it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options ) .
    endif.


**-- Get Request Header Object Reference
    lo_facade ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

**-- Get Parameters
    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid
                                                              e_userrole  = lv_userrole
                                                              e_ccode     = lv_bukrs ).






    if lv_loginid is not initial.
      select single @abap_true from zapp_roles into @data(lv_role_exists) where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'ADMIN' and application = 'APP.07'.
      if sy-subrc is not initial.
        select single @abap_true from zapp_roles into @lv_role_exists where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'BUYER' and application = 'APP.07'.
        if sy-subrc is not initial.

          select single @abap_true from zapp_roles into @lv_role_exists
             where id = @lv_loginid and role = 'APPROVER' and application = 'APP.07'.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            data(approver) = 'X'.
            translate lv_loginid to upper case.
            select id, name from zrfq_user_master into @data(ls_ID) up to 1 rows
              where id = @lv_loginid
              order by id.
            endselect.
          endif.

        else.
          data(buyerrole) = 'X'.
          translate lv_loginid to upper case.
          select id, name from zrfq_user_master into @ls_ID up to 1 rows
            where id = @lv_loginid
            order by id.
          endselect.
        endif.
      else.
        data(admin) = 'X'.
      endif.
    endif.


    select
        a~nfaid,
        a~po_number,
        a~nfa_type,
        a~subject,
        a~buyer,
        a~scope,
        a~scope2,
        a~justification,
        a~grp_details,
        a~deviation,
        a~vdd,
        a~vendor_name,
        a~po_amount,
        a~nfa_amount,
        a~plant_code,
        a~plant_name,
        a~purchase_grp,
        a~del_date,
        a~actaul_com_date,
        a~term_cond,
        a~work_type,
        a~amount,
        a~contract_val,
        a~uom,
        a~warranty,
        a~remarks,
        a~vendor_status,
        a~currency,
        a~alevel,
        a~status,
        a~draft,
         a~createdon,
      a~createdat,
            ' ' as querysolve,
          ' ' as querychk
          from zrfq_wnfa as a
          where nfaid in @lr_nfaid
            and purchase_grp is not initial
            and grp_details is not initial
            and buyer is not initial
          into table @data(lt_rfq_wnfa).



    data :  lv_flag  type abap_bool.

    loop at lt_rfq_wnfa assigning field-symbol(<fs_wnfa>).
      select *
        into table @data(lt_query)
        from zgnfa_query_tab
        where nfaid = @<fs_wnfa>-nfaid
        order by primary key.
      if lt_query is initial.
        clear: <fs_wnfa>-querysolve,
     <fs_wnfa>-querychk.
      else.
        lv_flag = abap_true.
        loop at lt_query into data(ls_query).
          if ls_query-answer is initial.
            lv_flag = abap_false.
            exit.
          endif.
        endloop.
        if lv_flag = abap_true.
          <fs_wnfa>-querysolve = 'X'.
          clear <fs_wnfa>-querychk.
        else.
          clear <fs_wnfa>-querysolve.
          <fs_wnfa>-querychk = 'X'.
        endif.
      endif.
    endloop.


    if lt_RFQ_WNFA is not initial.
      sort lt_rfq_wnfa by nfaid.
      delete adjacent duplicates from lt_rfq_wnfa comparing nfaid.
      select * from zdepartment_det into table @data(lt_DEPARTMENT_DET) for all entries in @lt_RFQ_WNFA where pur_grp = @lt_RFQ_WNFA-purchase_grp.
      if sy-subrc is initial.
        select * from zgroup_details into table @data(lt_GROUP_DETAILS) for all entries in @lt_RFQ_WNFA where grp_id = @lt_RFQ_WNFA-grp_details+0(4).
        if sy-subrc is initial and lt_DEPARTMENT_DET is not initial.
          data lr_user_id type range of zrfq_user_master-id.
          loop at lt_department_det into data(ls_dept_ids).
            lr_user_id = value #( base lr_user_id sign = 'I' option = 'EQ'
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-hod_user_department+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-hod_scm+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-hod_project_dev+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-functional_hod_scm+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-project_controller+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-budget_controller+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-finance_controller+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-head_sc+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-chro+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-gcfo+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-ceo+0(10) )
                                  ( low = ls_dept_ids-mgr_scm+0(10) ) ).
          endloop.
          sort lr_user_id by low.
          delete adjacent duplicates from lr_user_id comparing low.
          if lr_user_id is not initial.
            select id, name from zrfq_user_master into table @data(lt_USER_MASTER) where id in @lr_user_id.
          endif.
          select id, name from zrfq_user_master  appending table @lt_user_master for all entries in @lt_rfq_wnfa where id = @lt_rfq_wnfa-buyer+0(10).
          loop at lt_RFQ_WNFA into data(ls_RFQ_WNFA).

            if buyerrole = 'X'.
              if ls_ID is not initial and  ls_ID-id <> ls_RFQ_WNFA-buyer.

                continue.
              endif.
            endif.
            if ls_RFQ_WNFA-draft is initial.
              read table lt_GROUP_DETAILS into data(ls_GROUP_DETAILS) with key grp_id = ls_RFQ_WNFA-grp_details.
              if sy-subrc is initial.
                case ls_RFQ_WNFA-alevel.
                  when ''.  lv_level = 'BUYER'.
                  when '0'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level1.
                  when '1'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level2.
                  when '2'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level3.
                  when '3'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level4.
                  when '4'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level5.
                  when '5'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level6.
                  when '6'. lv_level = ls_GROUP_DETAILS-level7.
                endcase.
                if lv_level is not initial.
                  read table lt_DEPARTMENT_DET into data(ls_DEPARTMENT_DET) with key pur_grp = ls_RFQ_WNFA-purchase_grp.
                  if sy-subrc is initial.
                    case lv_level.
                    when 'BUYER'.
                      lv_apppending = text-025.
                    when 'HOD-U'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into data(ls_USER_MASTER) with key id = ls_department_det-hod_user_department.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-hod_user_department } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'HOD-S'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-hod_scm.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-hod_scm } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }| .
                    when 'HEADSC'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-head_sc.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-head_sc } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'PRCTR'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-project_controller.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-project_controller } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'FICTR'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-finance_controller.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-finance_controller } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'GCFO'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-gcfo.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-gcfo } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'CEO'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-ceo.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-ceo } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'HOD-PD'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-hod_project_dev.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-hod_project_dev } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'MGR-SCM'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-mgr_scm.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-mgr_scm } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'FHODSCM'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-functional_hod_scm } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'BGTCTR'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-budget_controller.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-budget_controller } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                    when 'CHRO'.
                      read table lt_USER_MASTER into ls_USER_MASTER with key id = ls_department_det-chro.
                      lv_apppending = |{ ls_department_det-chro } { ls_USER_MASTER-name }|.
                  endcase.
                  endif.
                endif.
              endif.
            endif.

            if approver = 'X'.
*          DATA(
              if ls_ID is not initial and  ls_ID-name <> ls_USER_MASTER-name.

                continue.
              endif.
            endif.
            select edate, etime from  zwnfa_hist into @data(ls_his) up to 1 rows
              where nfaid = @ls_RFQ_WNFA-nfaid
              order by edate descending, etime descending.
            endselect.

            concatenate ls_his-edate ls_his-etime into data(lv_date) separated by space.
            CONCATENATE ls_RFQ_WNFA-createdon ls_RFQ_WNFA-createdat INTO DATA(lv_create) SEPARATED BY space.

            append value #( nfaid = |{ ls_RFQ_WNFA-nfaid alpha = out }| PurGrp = ls_RFQ_WNFA-purchase_grp Buyer = value #( lt_user_master[ id = ls_RFQ_WNFA-buyer ]-name optional ) status = ls_RFQ_WNFA-status
                            AppPending = lv_apppending
                            scope      = |{ ls_RFQ_WNFA-scope }{ ls_RFQ_WNFA-scope2 }|
                            pendings   = lv_date
                            CreationDate = lv_create
                            querychk   = ls_RFQ_WNFA-querychk
                            querysolve = ls_RFQ_WNFA-querysolve
            ) to et_entityset.

            clear lv_apppending.
          endloop.
        endif.
      endif.
    endif.


    if APPROVEr = 'X'.
      delete et_entityset where status ne 'UA'.
    endif.

    clear : buyerrole , approver.
    sort et_entityset by nfaid descending.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->HISTORYSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_HISTORY
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method historyset_get_entityset.
*    data(lv_nfaid) = it_key_tab[ name = 'NFAID' ]-value.
    data(lv_nfaid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    if lv_nfaid is not initial.
      select
        nfaid,
        alevel as level,
        action,
        remark,
         cast( edate as char( 8 ) ) as date,
         cast( etime as char( 8 ) ) as time
        from ZWNFA_HIST
        into table @et_entityset
        where nfaid = @lv_nfaid.
      if et_entityset is not initial.
        loop at et_entityset assigning field-symbol(<ls_entityset>).
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'A'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-026.
          endif.
          if <ls_entityset>-action = 'R'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-027.
          endif.
           if <ls_entityset>-action = 'S'.
            <ls_entityset>-action = text-028.
          endif.
          <ls_entityset>-date = |{ <ls_entityset>-date+4(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+6(2) }/{ <ls_entityset>-date+0(4) }|.
          <ls_entityset>-time = |{ <ls_entityset>-time+0(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+2(2) }:{ <ls_entityset>-time+4(2) }|.
        endloop.
        sort et_entityset by date ascending time ascending.
      endif.
    endif.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->ITEMDETAILSSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_ITEMDETAILS
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method itemdetailsset_get_entityset.

    data: r_pono       type range of ebeln,
          ls_pono      like line of r_pono,
          es_entityset like line of et_entityset,
          lo_facade    type ref to /iwbep/if_mgw_dp_int_facade,
          lv_loginid   type string,
          lv_logintype type char1.

    try.
        data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PoNo' ].
        data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        ls_pono-low = ls_selopt-low.
        ls_pono-sign   = 'I'.
        ls_pono-option = 'CP'.
        append ls_pono to r_pono.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.
    if line_exists( it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ] ).
      data(lv_nfaid) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ]-select_options[ 1 ]-low.
    endif.


**-- Get Request Header Object Reference
    lo_facade ?= /iwbep/if_mgw_conv_srv_runtime~get_dp_facade( ).

    zcl_supp_portal_utility=>read_header_parameter( exporting io_facade   = lo_facade
                                                    importing e_logintype = lv_logintype
                                                              e_loginid   = lv_loginid ).
    if r_pono is not  initial.
      select ebeln,
             lifnr,
             bsart,
             ekgrp,
             eq_eindt from ekko into table @data(lt_ekko) where ebeln in @r_pono.

      if lt_ekko is not initial.
        select distinct b~lifnr,
               b~sperr,
               b~sperm from ekko as a inner join lfa1 as b on b~lifnr = a~lifnr
                                  into table @data(lt_lfa1)
                                  where a~ebeln in @r_pono.

        select ekgrp,
               eknam from t024 into table @data(lt_T024)  for all entries in @lt_ekko
                                                       where ekgrp = @lt_ekko-ekgrp.

        select ebeln,
               ebelp,
               werks,
          netwr  from ekpo into table @data(lt_ekpo)
                                                       where ebeln in @r_pono.

        if lt_ekpo is not initial.
          select werks,
                 name1 from t001w into table @data(lt_t001w) for all entries in @lt_ekpo
                                                        where werks = @lt_ekpo-werks.
          select ebeln,
                 ebelp,
                 eindt from eket into table @data(lt_eket)
                                                       where ebeln in @r_pono.
        endif.
      endif.


      select id, name from zrfq_user_master into @data(ls_ZRFQ_USER_MASTER) up to 1 rows
        where id = @lv_loginid
        order by id.
      endselect.

      sort lt_ekpo by ebeln.
      loop at lt_ekpo into data(ls_ekpo).
        es_entityset-plantcode = ls_ekpo-werks.
        es_entityset-pono = ls_ekpo-ebeln.
        data(lv_amt) = es_entityset-poamt + ls_ekpo-netwr.

        read table lt_t001w into data(ls_t001w) with key werks = ls_ekpo-werks.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          es_entityset-plantname = ls_t001w-name1.
        endif.


        read table lt_ekko into data(ls_ekko) with key ebeln = ls_ekpo-ebeln.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          es_entityset-vendorname = |{ ls_ekko-lifnr alpha = out }|.

          es_entityset-worktype = ls_ekko-bsart.
          es_entityset-purchasegrp = ls_ekko-ekgrp.
*        es_entityset-purchasegrp = |{ ls_ekko-ekgrp alpha = out }|.

          read table lt_T024 into data(ls_t024) with key ekgrp = ls_ekko-ekgrp.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            es_entityset-purchasegrpname = ls_t024-eknam.
          endif.

          read table lt_lfa1 into data(ls_lfa1) with key lifnr = ls_ekko-lifnr.
          if sy-subrc = 0.
            if ls_lfa1-sperm = 'X' and ls_lfa1-sperr = 'X'.
              es_entityset-vendorstatus = text-029.
            else.
              es_entityset-vendorstatus = text-030.
            endif.
          endif.


        endif.

        read table lt_eket into data(ls_eket) with key ebeln = ls_ekKo-ebeln.
        if sy-subrc = 0.
          es_entityset-deliverydate = ls_eket-eindt.
        endif.

        es_entityset-buyer = ls_ZRFQ_USER_MASTER-name.
        es_entityset-buyerid = ls_ZRFQ_USER_MASTER-id.
*      es_entityset-buyer = ls_ZRFQ_USER_MASTER-name.

        at end of ebeln.
          es_entityset-poamt = lv_amt.
          append es_entityset to et_entityset.
          clear es_entityset.
        endat.
      endloop.
    endif.
    if lv_nfaid is not initial.

      select
  a~po_number,
  a~vendor_name,
  a~po_amount,
  a~nfa_amount,
  a~plant_code,
  a~plant_name,
  a~purchase_grp,
  a~del_date,
  a~actaul_com_date,
  a~term_cond,
  a~work_type,
  a~Amount,
  a~contract_val,
  a~warranty,
  a~Remarks,
  a~vendor_status
  from zrfq_wnfa as a
  into table @data(lT_ITEM)
  where nfaid = @lv_nfaid.
      if lt_item is not initial.
        select ekgrp, eknam from t024 into table @data(lt_t024_grp)
          for all entries in @lt_item
          where ekgrp = @lt_item-purchase_grp.
        loop at lt_item into data(ls_item_chk).
          if not line_exists( lt_t024_grp[ ekgrp = ls_item_chk-purchase_grp ] ).
            delete lt_item.
          endif.
        endloop.
      endif.

      et_entityset = value #( for ls_item1 in lt_item (
                              PoNo            = ls_item1-po_number
                              VendorName      = ls_item1-vendor_name
                              POAmt           = ls_item1-po_amount
                              NFAAmt          = ls_item1-nfa_amount
                              PlantCode       = ls_item1-plant_code
                              PlantName       = ls_item1-plant_name
                              PurchaseGrp     = ls_item1-purchase_grp
                              purchasegrpname = value #( lt_t024_grp[ ekgrp = ls_item1-purchase_grp ]-eknam optional )
                              DeliveryDate    = ls_item1-del_date
                              ActualWord      = ls_item1-actaul_com_date
                              Termcond        = ls_item1-term_cond
                              WorkType        = ls_item1-work_type
                              Amount          = ls_item1-Amount
                              ContractVal     = ls_item1-contract_val
                              WarrantyDLP     = ls_item1-warranty
                              Remarks         = ls_item1-Remarks
                              VendorStatus    = ls_item1-vendor_status ) ).
    endif.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->NFAIDHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_NFAIDHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method nfaidhelpset_get_entityset.
    data : es_entityset type ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_mpc=>ts_nfaidhelp.
    data : lv_filter type string.
    if iv_search_string is not initial.
      lv_filter = |%{ iv_search_string }%|.
      select nfaid from zrfq_wnfa where draft is initial and nfaid like @lv_filter into table @data(lt_item) .
    else.
      select nfaid from zrfq_wnfa where draft is initial  into table @lt_item.
    endif.
    sort lt_item by  nfaid descending.
    delete adjacent duplicates from lt_item comparing nfaid.
    loop at lt_item into data(ls_item).
      es_entityset-nfaid = |{ ls_item-nfaid alpha = out }|.
      append es_entityset to et_entityset.
      clear es_entityset.
    endloop.

  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->PONUMHELPSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_PONUMHELP
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
    method ponumhelpset_get_entityset.

      data : lv_search_string type string,
             lt_po            type range of char12,
             ls_po            like line of lt_po,
             lv_top           type string,
             lv_skip          type i.

      if iv_search_string is not initial.
        lv_search_string = iv_search_string.
        translate lv_search_string to upper case.
        concatenate '*' lv_search_string '*' into lv_search_string.

        ls_po-sign = 'I'.
        ls_po-option = 'CP'.
        ls_po-low = lv_search_string.
        append ls_po to lt_po.
        clear ls_po.

      endif.

      data : r_ekgrp type range of ekko-ekgrp,
             l_ekgrp like line of r_ekgrp.
      try.
          data(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'PurchaseGroup' ].
          data(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
          l_ekgrp-low = ls_selopt-low.
          l_ekgrp-sign   = 'I'.
          l_ekgrp-option = 'EQ'.
          append  l_ekgrp to r_ekgrp.
          clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
        catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
      endtry.

**- Get number of records requested
      clear: lv_top,lv_skip.
      lv_top = io_tech_request_context->get_top( ).

**- Get number of lines that should be skipped
      lv_skip = io_tech_request_context->get_skip( ).
      lv_top = lv_top + lv_skip.

      select ekgrp ebeln from ekko into table et_entityset UP TO lv_Top ROWS where ekgrp in r_ekgrp and ebeln in lt_po
        order by ebeln.

      if lv_skip is not initial and lv_skip ne 0.
        delete et_entityset from 1 to lv_skip.
      endif.


    endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->PURCHASEGRP_F4SE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_PURCHASEGRP_F4
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method purchasegrp_f4se_get_entityset.
    data : lv_ekgrp type eban-ekgrp.

    data : r_ekgrp type range of char20,
           l_ekgrp like line of r_ekgrp,
           lv_search_string type string.

        lv_search_string = iv_search_string.
        translate lv_search_string to upper case.
        concatenate '*' lv_search_string '*' into lv_search_string.

        l_ekgrp-sign = 'I'.
        l_ekgrp-option = 'CP'.
        l_ekgrp-low = lv_search_string.
        append l_ekgrp  to r_ekgrp.
        clear l_ekgrp.

*    lv_ekgrp = iv_search_string.

    if r_ekgrp is not initial.
      select ekgrp, eknam from t024 into table @data(lt_t024)
        where ekgrp in @r_ekgrp or eknam in @r_ekgrp.
    else.
      select ekgrp, eknam from t024 into table @lt_t024
        where ekgrp <> @space.
    endif.

    if lt_t024 is not initial.
      select distinct ekgrp from eban into table @data(lt_eban_grp)
        where ekgrp <> @space.
      loop at lt_t024 into data(ls_t024).
        if line_exists( lt_eban_grp[ ekgrp = ls_t024-ekgrp ] ).
          append value #( purchasegroup = ls_t024-ekgrp
                          text          = ls_t024-eknam ) to et_entityset.
        endif.
      endloop.
    endif.

    sort et_entityset ascending.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_QUERYSET
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method QUERYSETSET_GET_ENTITYSET.


data : r_nfaid    type range of znfa_header-nfa_id,
           l_nfaid    like line of r_nfaid.

    try.
        DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
        DATA(ls_selopt) = ls_filter-select_options[ 1 ].
        l_NFAID-low = |{ ls_selopt-low ALPHA = IN }|.
        l_NFAID-sign   = 'I'.
        l_NFAID-option = 'EQ'.
        append  l_NFAID to r_NFAID.
        clear : ls_filter,ls_selopt.
      catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
    endtry.

    select nfaid,
           eventid ,
           CONCAT( question, question1 ) as question,
           CONCAT( answer, answer1 ) as answer,
           querychk,
           raisedby,
           raisedon
       from zgnfa_query_tab
      where nfaid in @r_nfaid
      into corresponding fields of table @et_entityset.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->RECALLNFASET_CREATE_ENTITY
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITY_C(optional)
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IO_DATA_PROVIDER               TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_ENTRY_PROVIDER(optional)
* | [<---] ER_ENTITY                      TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TS_RECALLNFA
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method RECALLNFASET_CREATE_ENTITY.
DATA : LS_DATA TYPE ZCL_ZRFQ_GENERALNFA_PR_MPC=>ts_recallnfa.

data(NFAID) = VALUE #( IT_KEY_TAB[ NAME = 'NFAID' ]-value OPTIONAL ).

IO_DATA_PROVIDER->read_entry_data(
  importing
    es_data = LS_DATA
).
*catch /iwbep/cx_mgw_tech_exception. " mgw technical exception

DATA : lv_action TYPE zwnfa_hist-action,
       lv_remark TYPE zwnfa_hist-remark,
       lv_nfaid  TYPE zwnfa_hist-nfaid.
lv_action = 'RC'.
lv_remark = LS_DATA-remark.
lv_nfaid  = LS_DATA-NFAID.

UPDATE ZWNFA_HIST SET ACTION = @lv_action,
                         edate = @SY-DATLO,
                         etime  = @SY-uzeit,
                         remark = @lv_remark
WHERE NFAID = @lv_nfaid.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
UPDATE zrfq_wnfa SET draft = 'X',
                        status = 'D',
                        alevel = ' '
                        WHERE NFAID = @LS_DATA-NFAID.
IF SY-SUBRC = 0.
COMMIT WORK.
ENDIF.
  endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Public Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->SEND_MAIL
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] I_APPROVER                     TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_REASON                       TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_BUYER                        TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_NFAID                        TYPE        CHAR10(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILTO                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_EMAILCC                      TYPE        TT_EMAIL(optional)
* | [--->] I_SUBJECT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [--->] I_CONTENT                      TYPE        STRING(optional)
* | [<---] RETURN                         TYPE        CHAR10
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
method send_mail.
  types: begin of ty_email,
           email type ad_smtpadr,
         end of ty_email.

  data: lt_email type table of ty_email,
        ls_email type ty_email.

  data: lt_mailrecipients type standard table of somlrec90,
        ls_mailrecipients type somlrec90,
        ls_mailsubject    type sodocchgi1,
        lt_packing_list   type standard table of sopcklsti1,
        ls_packing_list   type sopcklsti1,
        gv_cnt            type i,
        lv_partner        type bu_partner,
        lv_vendor         type lifnr,
        wa_spfli          type spfli.

  data: addsmtp   type table of bapiadsmtp,
        it_return type table of bapiret2.

  data:lv_month(2) type c,
       lv_year(4)  type c.
  data:lv_date type char10.
  data:lv_date1 type datum.

  data: lo_send_request  type ref to  cl_bcs,
        lo_document      type ref to  cl_document_bcs,
        lo_recipient     type ref to  if_recipient_bcs,
        lo_bcs_exception type ref to  cx_bcs,
        lt_mailtxt       type standard table of solisti1,
        l_mailtxt        type solisti1,
        sender1          type ref to cl_cam_address_bcs,
        lv_sent_to_all   type os_boolean,
        lv_subject       type char50,
        lv_nfaid         type char10.

  data: lo_sender        type ref to if_sender_bcs value is initial. " Added by shahrukh 08/01/2026.

  constants: lc_br  type string value '</br>',
             lc_br2 type string value '<br/>'.

  if i_nfaid is not initial.
    lv_nfaid = |{ i_nfaid alpha = out }|.
  endif.
  case i_content.
    when 'MAIL1'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_approver },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-031 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
        ( line = text-032 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = |<a href="https://azureportalprod-cqtz7hbe.launchpad.cfapps.in30.hana.ondemand.com/site?siteId=fb5c783b-a296-47c9-b4d3-a1a5d4285ace#Shell-home">Click here to view</a></br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-033 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-034 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL2'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-035 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-036 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-037 )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-038 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL3'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
  ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
  ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
  ( line = |<br/>| )
  ( line = replace( val = text-039 sub = '&1' with = lv_nfaid ) && lc_br )
  ( line = text-040 && lc_br )
  ( line = |<br/>| )
  ( line = text-033 && lc_br2 )
  ( line = text-034 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL5'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = replace( val = text-041 sub = '&1' with = i_nfaid ) sub = '&2' with = i_approver ) && lc_br )
        ( line = |{ i_reason }.</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-033 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-034 )
      ).
    when 'MAIL6'.
      lt_mailtxt = value #(
        ( line = |<HTML> <BODY>| )
        ( line = |<p style="font-family:Calibri;font-size:15;">Dear { i_buyer },</br>| )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = replace( val = text-042 sub = '&1' with = i_approver ) && lc_br )
        ( line = text-043 && lc_br )
        ( line = |<br/>| )
        ( line = text-033 && lc_br2 )
        ( line = text-034 )
      ).
  endcase.

  try.
*     -------- create persistent send request ------------------------
      lo_send_request = cl_bcs=>create_persistent( ).

*     -------- create and set document -------------------------------
      lv_subject = i_subject.
      lo_document = cl_document_bcs=>create_document(
        i_type    = 'HTM'
        i_text    = lt_mailtxt
        i_subject = lv_subject ).


      data:lv_send type string.
      lv_send  = sy-uname.
      call method lo_send_request->set_document( lo_document ).

      try.
          lo_sender = cl_sapuser_bcs=>create( sy-uname ).
          lo_send_request->set_sender(
            exporting
              i_sender = lo_sender ).
        catch cx_address_bcs.
****Catch exception here
      endtry.
      """""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""""EOC.


      loop at i_emailto into ls_email.
        if ls_email-email is not initial.
          lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
            ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
          call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
            exporting
              i_recipient = lo_recipient
              i_express   = 'X'.
          clear ls_email.
        endif.
      endloop.
      loop at i_emailcc into ls_email.
        if ls_email-email is not initial.
          lo_recipient = cl_cam_address_bcs=>create_internet_address(
            ls_email-email ).

*     add recipient with its respective attributes to send request
          call method lo_send_request->add_recipient
            exporting
              i_recipient = lo_recipient
              i_copy      = 'X'.
          clear ls_email.
        endif.
      endloop.

      call method lo_send_request->set_send_immediately
        exporting
          i_send_immediately = 'X'.

      call method lo_send_request->send(
        exporting
          i_with_error_screen = 'X'
        receiving
          result              = lv_sent_to_all ).
      if lv_sent_to_all is initial.
        return = 'X'.
      else.
        commit work and wait.
      endif.
    catch cx_bcs into lo_bcs_exception.
  endtry.
endmethod.


* <SIGNATURE>---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | Instance Protected Method ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_DPC_EXT->WNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET
* +-------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------+
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_ENTITY_SET_NAME             TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SOURCE_NAME                 TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IT_FILTER_SELECT_OPTIONS       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SELECT_OPTION
* | [--->] IS_PAGING                      TYPE        /IWBEP/S_MGW_PAGING
* | [--->] IT_KEY_TAB                     TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAME_VALUE_PAIR
* | [--->] IT_NAVIGATION_PATH             TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_NAVIGATION_PATH
* | [--->] IT_ORDER                       TYPE        /IWBEP/T_MGW_SORTING_ORDER
* | [--->] IV_FILTER_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IV_SEARCH_STRING               TYPE        STRING
* | [--->] IO_TECH_REQUEST_CONTEXT        TYPE REF TO /IWBEP/IF_MGW_REQ_ENTITYSET(optional)
* | [<---] ET_ENTITYSET                   TYPE        ZCL_ZWNFA_PROCESS_MPC=>TT_WNFAATTACHMENT
* | [<---] ES_RESPONSE_CONTEXT            TYPE        /IWBEP/IF_MGW_APPL_SRV_RUNTIME=>TY_S_MGW_RESPONSE_CONTEXT
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_BUSI_EXCEPTION
* | [!CX!] /IWBEP/CX_MGW_TECH_EXCEPTION
* +--------------------------------------------------------------------------------------</SIGNATURE>
  method WNFAATTACHMENTSE_GET_ENTITYSET.
data : r_NFAID  type RANGE OF  ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT-NFAID,
           l_NFAID  like line of r_NFAID,
           er_entity like LINE OF et_entityset.

    try.
            DATA(ls_filter) = it_filter_select_options[ property = 'NFAID' ].
           loop at ls_filter-select_options INTO DATA(ls_selopt).
            l_NFAid-low = ls_selopt-low.
            l_NFAid-sign   = 'I'.
            l_NFAid-option = 'EQ'.
            append  l_NFAid to r_NFAid.
            clear : ls_selopt.
            ENDLOOP.
            clear : ls_filter.
          catch cx_sy_itab_line_not_found.
        endtry.


SELECT FROM ZRFQ_ATTACHMENT

  FIELDS
  RFQID,
  NFAID,
  FILE_NAME,
  UPLOADED_BY,
  DOKNR,
  Specification,
  file_type,
  SENDTOSUPPLIER,
  SENDTOPO
  WHERE NFAID IN @r_NFAid and attachmenttype = 'WNFA'
  INTO  TABLE @DATA(IT_DATA).

et_entityset =  VALUE #( for LS_DATA IN IT_DATA INDEX INTO IND (

                         SRNO = IND
                         Specification = ls_data-Specification
                         FileName = ls_data-FILE_NAME
                         FileType = ls_data-file_type
                         DocID  = ls_data-DOKNR
                         SendToSupplier = ls_data-SENDTOSUPPLIER
                         RFQID = ls_data-rfqid
                         SENDTOPO = LS_DATA-sendtopo
                         NFAID  = LS_DATA-nfaid

                         ) ).
  endmethod.
ENDCLASS.
